 - **`TelemetryAnalyzer`** (src/core/telemetry_analyzer.py): Processes FrameData into analysis
 - **`APIClient`** (src/core/api_client.py): Handles lap submissions to server
 
+### Data Sources (Corrected Analysis)
+- **Game Logs**: 10 event patterns (player identification, sector splits, lap completion events, penalties)
+  - **Exclusive to logs**: Steam ID/car UUID, sector split times (race/practice modes), lap completion events with timestamps
+- **Shared Memory (3 regions)**:
+  - **Static SHM**: Session metadata (track, version, event IDs, weather, session rules)
+  - **Graphics SHM**: Real-time lap timing (current_laptime, last_laptime, best_laptime, ideal_laptime), validity flags (is_invalid), fuel data (fuel_liter_current_quantity, fuel_liter_per_km, km_per_fuel_liter), session state, race position
+  - **Physics SHM**: Detailed vehicle dynamics, forces, temperatures, wear, aero data
+- **Overlap**: Session metadata (track, game version, session type), basic timing (lap times exist in both), validity (is_invalid in SHM, penalty events in logs)
+
 ### Data Flow Issues
-1. **Duplication**: Both log parser and telemetry analyzer maintain separate lap timing data
-2. **Inconsistent sources**: Different validity flags between logs and telemetry
-3. **Missing setup data**: Car setup scattered across different components
-4. **No unified interface**: Each system has its own data access patterns
+1. **Duplication**: Both log parser and telemetry analyzer maintain separate lap timing data (SHM Graphics already provides this)
+2. **Inconsistent sources**: Different validity flags between logs and telemetry (SHM Graphics provides is_invalid)
+3. **Missing SHM regions**: Static SHM not currently utilized for session metadata
+4. **Over-reliance on logs**: SHM Graphics provides lap timing and fuel data, but system still prioritizes logs
+5. **No unified interface**: Each system has its own data access patterns
 
 ## 🏗️ Proposed Architecture
 
+### Shared Data Objects Plan
+
+The unified system requires specific data objects to be shared across components. These objects replace the current fragmented data access patterns:
+
+#### 1. **LapValidityData** (Critical for Lap Posting)
+**Current State**: Log parser extracts validity from penalty events and track limit violations
+**Target State**: Graphics SHM provides `is_invalid` flag for current lap
+
+```python
+@dataclass
+class LapValidityData:
+    """Lap validity information for lap posting"""
+    lap_number: int
+    is_valid: bool
+    invalidation_reason: Optional[str] = None  # e.g., "track_limits", "penalty"
+    invalidation_timestamp: Optional[str] = None
+    source: str = "shm_graphics"  # or "logs" as fallback
+
+    # For historical laps (from logs)
+    penalty_count: Optional[int] = None
+    track_limit_violations: Optional[int] = None
+```
+
+**Usage:**
+- **Telemetry Analyzer**: Uses `is_valid` from Graphics SHM for current lap validity
+- **API Client**: Posts `is_valid` field with lap submissions
+- **Log Parser**: Fallback to log-based validity for historical laps
+- **Priority**: Graphics SHM (real-time) > Logs (historical)
+
+#### 2. **LapTimingData**
+**Current State**: Both log parser and telemetry analyzer calculate lap times separately
+**Target State**: Graphics SHM provides definitive lap timing
+
+```python
+@dataclass
+class LapTimingData:
+    """Lap timing information from Graphics SHM"""
+    lap_number: int
+    current_lap_time_ms: Optional[int] = None
+    last_lap_time_ms: Optional[int] = None
+    best_lap_time_ms: Optional[int] = None
+    ideal_lap_time_ms: Optional[int] = None
+    delta_time_ms: Optional[int] = None
+    source: str = "shm_graphics"  # or "logs" as fallback
+
+    # For historical laps (from logs)
+    lap_time_str: Optional[str] = None  # formatted string from logs
+    lap_completion_timestamp: Optional[str] = None
+```
+
+**Usage:**
+- **Telemetry Analyzer**: Uses Graphics SHM timing for real-time lap detection
+- **API Client**: Posts lap times with submissions
+- **Log Parser**: Provides historical lap completion events
+- **Priority**: Graphics SHM (real-time) > Logs (completion events)
+
+#### 3. **FuelData**
+**Current State**: Log parser calculates fuel consumption per lap
+**Target State**: Graphics SHM provides real-time fuel data
+
+```python
+@dataclass
+class FuelData:
+    """Fuel information from Graphics SHM"""
+    current_fuel: Optional[float] = None  # litres
+    fuel_consumption_rate: Optional[float] = None  # litres per km
+    fuel_economy: Optional[float] = None  # km per litre
+    fuel_consumed_lap: Optional[float] = None  # litres per lap (from logs)
+    source: str = "shm_graphics"  # or "logs" for per-lap data
+```
+
+**Usage:**
+- **Telemetry Analyzer**: Uses Graphics SHM fuel for real-time monitoring
+- **API Client**: Posts fuel data with lap submissions
+- **Log Parser**: Provides per-lap fuel consumption
+- **Priority**: Graphics SHM (real-time) > Logs (per-lap calculation)
+
+#### 4. **PlayerIdentificationData**
+**Current State**: Log parser extracts Steam ID and car UUID from connection events
+**Target State**: Remains log-only (not available in SHM)
+
+```python
+@dataclass
+class PlayerIdentificationData:
+    """Player identification from logs (SHM doesn't provide this)"""
+    steam_id: Optional[str] = None
+    player_name: Optional[str] = None
+    car_uuid: Optional[str] = None
+    car_model: Optional[str] = None
+    source: str = "logs"  # ONLY from logs
+```
+
+**Usage:**
+- **API Client**: Posts player identification with lap submissions
+- **Log Parser**: Extracts from connection events
+- **Priority**: Logs ONLY (no SHM alternative)
+
+#### 5. **SectorSplitData**
+**Current State**: Log parser extracts sector times from split events
+**Target State**: Remains log-only (SHM doesn't provide sector breakdown)
+
+```python
+@dataclass
+class SectorSplitData:
+    """Sector split times from logs (SHM doesn't provide sector breakdown)"""
+    lap_number: int
+    sector1_ms: Optional[int] = None
+    sector2_ms: Optional[int] = None
+    sector3_ms: Optional[int] = None
+    source: str = "logs"  # ONLY from logs
+```
+
+**Usage:**
+- **Telemetry Analyzer**: Uses sector times for corner analysis
+- **API Client**: Posts sector data with lap submissions
+- **Log Parser**: Extracts from race_split/practice_split events
+- **Priority**: Logs ONLY (no SHM alternative)
+
+#### 6. **SessionMetadataData**
+**Current State**: Log parser extracts session metadata from game started events
+**Target State**: Static SHM provides authoritative session metadata
+
+```python
+@dataclass
+class SessionMetadataData:
+    """Session metadata from Static SHM"""
+    session_id: str
+    game_version: str
+    session_type: str
+    session_name: str
+    track: str
+    track_configuration: str
+    track_length_m: Optional[float] = None
+    weather: str = "Unknown"
+    is_online: bool = False
+    is_timed_race: bool = False
+    event_id: Optional[int] = None
+    source: str = "shm_static"  # or "logs" as fallback
+```
+
+**Usage:**
+- **API Client**: Posts session metadata with lap submissions
+- **Telemetry Analyzer**: Uses for track-based analysis
+- **Log Parser**: Provides fallback session metadata
+- **Priority**: Static SHM > Logs
+
+### Data Object Integration Flow
+
+```
+┌─────────────────┐
+│   Log Parser    │
+│  (player ID,    │
+│   sectors,      │
+│   lap events)   │
+└────────┬────────┘
+         │
+         ▼
+┌─────────────────────────────────┐
+│      SharedSessionManager        │
+│  ┌─────────────────────────┐    │
+│  │ LapValidityData         │    │
+│  │ (from Graphics SHM)      │    │
+│  └─────────────────────────┘    │
+│  ┌─────────────────────────┐    │
+│  │ LapTimingData           │    │
+│  │ (from Graphics SHM)      │    │
+│  └─────────────────────────┘    │
+│  ┌─────────────────────────┐    │
+│  │ FuelData                │    │
+│  │ (from Graphics SHM)      │    │
+│  └─────────────────────────┘    │
+│  ┌─────────────────────────┐    │
+│  │ PlayerIdentificationData│   │
+│  │ (from Logs ONLY)        │    │
+│  └─────────────────────────┘    │
+│  ┌─────────────────────────┐    │
+│  │ SectorSplitData         │    │
+│  │ (from Logs ONLY)        │    │
+│  └─────────────────────────┘    │
+│  ┌─────────────────────────┐    │
+│  │ SessionMetadataData     │    │
+│  │ (from Static SHM)       │    │
+│  └─────────────────────────┘    │
+└────────┬────────────────────────┘
+         │
+         ▼
+┌─────────────────┐
+│  API Client     │
+│  (posts laps    │
+│   with shared   │
+│   data objects) │
+└─────────────────┘
+```
+
+### Critical Transition: Lap Validity
+
+**Current Flow (Log-Based):**
+```
+Log Parser → detects penalty events → sets is_valid=False → API Client posts lap
+```
+
+**Target Flow (SHM-Based):**
+```
+Graphics SHM → provides is_invalid flag → SharedSessionManager → API Client posts lap
+```
+
+**Implementation Steps:**
+1. Add `LapValidityData` to SharedSessionData
+2. Update Graphics SHM decoder to extract `is_invalid` from SMEvoTimingState
+3. Update SharedSessionManager to store validity from Graphics SHM
+4. Update API Client to use `LapValidityData.is_valid` for lap posting
+5. Keep log-based validity as fallback for historical laps
+6. Add validation to ensure consistency between SHM and log validity
+

+### Phase 3: Graphics SHM Integration (Lap Timing & Fuel)
+
+#### 3.1 Update Graphics SHM Decoder
+**File**: `src/core/telemetry_decoder.py`
+```python
+def decode_graphics_evo(data: bytes) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
+    """Decode the AC Evo SPageFileGraphicEvo shared-memory region."""
+    # Extract SMEvoTimingState for lap timing
+    timing_state = {
+        "current_laptime": ...,
+        "last_laptime": ...,
+        "best_laptime": ...,
+        "ideal_laptime": ...,
+        "delta_current": ...,
+        "delta_last": ...,
+        "total_time": ...,
+        "is_invalid": ...,
+    }
+
+    # Extract fuel data
+    fuel_data = {
+        "fuel_liter_current_quantity": ...,
+        "fuel_liter_per_km": ...,
+        "km_per_fuel_liter": ...,
+    }
+
+    return {**timing_state, **fuel_data, ...}
+```

+#### 3.2 Update Session Manager for Graphics SHM
+**File**: `src/models/shared_session.py`
+```python
+def update_lap_validity_from_graphics_shm(self, lap_num: int, is_invalid: bool):
+    """Update lap validity from Graphics SHM (critical for lap posting)"""
+    with self._lock:
+        # Create or update LapValidityData object
+        if lap_num not in self._session_data.lap_validity:
+            self._session_data.lap_validity[lap_num] = LapValidityData(
+                lap_number=lap_num,
+                is_valid=not is_invalid,
+                source="shm_graphics"
+            )
+        else:
+            self._session_data.lap_validity[lap_num].is_valid = not is_invalid
+            self._session_data.lap_validity[lap_num].source = "shm_graphics"
+
+        # Update legacy flat field for backward compatibility
+        self._session_data.lap_validity_flat[lap_num] = not is_invalid
+        self._session_data.is_current_lap_invalid = is_invalid
+
+        # Track data source
+        self._session_data.data_sources["lap_validity"].add("shm_graphics")
+
+        self.notify_observers()
+
+def update_from_graphics_shm(self, graphics_data: Dict):
+    """Update lap timing and fuel data from Graphics shared memory"""
+    with self._lock:
+        # Lap timing from SMEvoTimingState
+        current_lap = graphics_data.get("session_current_lap", 0)
+        self._session_data.current_lap_time_ms = graphics_data.get("current_laptime_ms")
+        self._session_data.last_lap_time_ms = graphics_data.get("last_laptime_ms")
+        self._session_data.best_lap_time_ms = graphics_data.get("best_laptime_ms")
+        self._session_data.ideal_lap_time_ms = graphics_data.get("ideal_laptime_ms")
+        self._session_data.delta_time_ms = graphics_data.get("delta_time_ms")
+
+        # Update LapTimingData object for current lap
+        if current_lap not in self._session_data.lap_timing:
+            self._session_data.lap_timing[current_lap] = LapTimingData(
+                lap_number=current_lap,
+                current_lap_time_ms=graphics_data.get("current_laptime_ms"),
+                last_lap_time_ms=graphics_data.get("last_laptime_ms"),
+                best_lap_time_ms=graphics_data.get("best_laptime_ms"),
+                ideal_lap_time_ms=graphics_data.get("ideal_laptime_ms"),
+                delta_time_ms=graphics_data.get("delta_time_ms"),
+                source="shm_graphics"
+            )
+        else:
+            # Update existing timing data
+            self._session_data.lap_timing[current_lap].current_lap_time_ms = graphics_data.get("current_laptime_ms")
+            self._session_data.lap_timing[current_lap].last_lap_time_ms = graphics_data.get("last_laptime_ms")
+            self._session_data.lap_timing[current_lap].best_lap_time_ms = graphics_data.get("best_laptime_ms")
+
+        # Update lap validity (critical for lap posting)
+        is_invalid = graphics_data.get("is_invalid", False)
+        self.update_lap_validity_from_graphics_shm(current_lap, is_invalid)
+
+        # Fuel data
+        self._session_data.current_fuel = graphics_data.get("fuel_liter_current_quantity")
+        self._session_data.fuel_consumption_rate = graphics_data.get("fuel_liter_per_km")
+        self._session_data.fuel_economy = graphics_data.get("km_per_fuel_liter")
+
+        # Update FuelData object
+        self._session_data.fuel_data.current_fuel = graphics_data.get("fuel_liter_current_quantity")
+        self._session_data.fuel_data.fuel_consumption_rate = graphics_data.get("fuel_liter_per_km")
+        self._session_data.fuel_data.fuel_economy = graphics_data.get("km_per_fuel_liter")
+        self._session_data.fuel_data.source = "shm_graphics"
+
+        # Session state
+        self._session_data.total_laps = graphics_data.get("total_lap_count")
+        self._session_data.current_lap = graphics_data.get("session_current_lap")
+        self._session_data.current_pos = graphics_data.get("current_pos")
+        self._session_data.total_drivers = graphics_data.get("total_drivers")
+
+        # Track data sources
+        for field in ["current_lap_time_ms", "last_lap_time_ms", "best_lap_time_ms",
+                     "is_current_lap_invalid", "current_fuel", "fuel_consumption_rate"]:
+            self._session_data.data_sources[field].add("shm_graphics")
+
+        self.notify_observers()
+```

+def update_session_metadata_from_static_shm(self, metadata: Dict):

 ```python
 def get_lap_time(self, lap_num: int) -> Optional[float]:
     """Get lap time with definitive source priority"""

+

+def get_current_lap_time(self) -> Optional[int]:
+    """Get current lap time from Graphics SHM"""
+    with self._lock:
+        return self._session_data.current_lap_time_ms
+
+def get_fuel_data(self) -> Dict[str, Optional[float]]:
+    """Get fuel data from Graphics SHM"""
+    with self._lock:
+        return {
+            "current_fuel": self._session_data.current_fuel,
+            "consumption_rate": self._session_data.fuel_consumption_rate,
+            "economy": self._session_data.fuel_economy
+        }
+
+def get_session_metadata(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    """Get session metadata with priority: Static SHM > Logs > Graphics SHM"""
+    with self._lock:
+        metadata = {}
+        sources = self._session_data.data_sources
+
+        # Priority: Static SHM > Logs > Graphics SHM
+        for field in ["session_id", "game_version", "track", "weather", "session_type"]:
+            if "shm_static" in sources.get(field, set()):
+                metadata[field] = getattr(self._session_data, field)
+            elif "logs" in sources.get(field, set()):
+                metadata[field] = getattr(self._session_data, field)
+            elif "shm_graphics" in sources.get(field, set()):
+                metadata[field] = getattr(self._session_data, field)
+
+        # Player ID ONLY from logs (Steam ID, car UUID not in SHM)
+        metadata["player_id"] = self._session_data.player_id
+        metadata["car_uuid"] = self._session_data.car_uuid
+
+        return metadata
+
+def get_sector_times(self, lap_num: int) -> Optional[Dict[int, int]]:
+    """Get sector times - ONLY from logs (SHM doesn't provide sector breakdown)"""
+    with self._lock:
+        return self._session_data.sector_times.get(lap_num)
 ```

 ```python
 ## 🚀 Risk Mitigation
 
@@ -360,3 +967,46 @@ src/models/context.py (merge functionality)
 ```
 
 This implementation plan provides a comprehensive roadmap for creating a unified session data system that eliminates duplication while maintaining data integrity and performance.
+
+## 📝 Amendments Based on Corrected Data Analysis
+
+**Updated Statistics (from actual data gathering):**
+- **Log Patterns Found**: 10 event patterns (lap_finish, lap_validity, race_split, practice_split, physics_lap, penalty, connect, driver_line, version, track_name_direct)
+- **SHM Physics Fields**: 96 fields (85 documented + 11 unexpected)
+- **SHM Graphics Fields**: 89 fields (75 documented + 14 unexpected, includes lap timing and fuel data)
+- **SHM Static Fields**: 29 fields (19 documented + 10 unexpected)
+
+**Critical Corrections:**
+1. **SHM Graphics provides lap timing**: current_laptime, last_laptime, best_laptime, ideal_laptime, delta times, is_invalid flag
+2. **SHM Graphics provides fuel data**: fuel_liter_current_quantity, fuel_liter_per_km, km_per_fuel_liter
+3. **Logs are ONLY needed for**: Player identification (Steam ID, car UUID), sector split times, lap completion events
+4. **Revised priority system**: Graphics SHM > Logs for lap timing and fuel, Static SHM > Logs > Graphics SHM for session metadata, Logs ONLY for player ID and sectors
+5. **Reduced log dependency**: Most data now available from SHM, logs only for player ID and sector breakdown
+
+**Data Source Priority Rationale:**
+- **Graphics SHM**: Real-time lap timing (current, last, best, ideal), validity flags, fuel consumption rate, fuel economy
+- **Static SHM**: Authoritative session metadata (track, version, event IDs, weather, session rules)
+- **Logs**: Player identification (Steam ID, car UUID) - NOT available in SHM, sector split times - NOT available in SHM, lap completion events with timestamps
+- **Physics SHM**: Detailed telemetry, forces, temperatures, aero data
+- **Calculated**: Derived metrics when no source available
+
+**Key Insight:**
+SHM provides comprehensive real-time data including lap timing and fuel, eliminating most dependencies on logs. Logs are now only essential for player identification and sector split times, which SHM doesn't provide. This significantly reduces the criticality of log parsing for most session data needs.
+
+**Shared Data Objects Addition:**
+Added comprehensive plan for shared data objects to replace fragmented data access:
+- **LapValidityData**: Critical for lap posting, now from Graphics SHM instead of logs
+- **LapTimingData**: Real-time lap timing from Graphics SHM
+- **FuelData**: Real-time fuel data from Graphics SHM
+- **PlayerIdentificationData**: Player ID from logs ONLY (no SHM alternative)
+- **SectorSplitData**: Sector splits from logs ONLY (no SHM alternative)
+- **SessionMetadataData**: Session metadata from Static SHM
+
+**Critical Transition - Lap Validity:**
+Previously: Log parser detected penalty events → set is_valid → API Client posted lap
+Now: Graphics SHM provides is_invalid flag → SharedSessionManager → API Client posts lap
+
+This ensures real-time validity detection for lap posting and eliminates the dependency on log parsing for lap validity.
+
+This plan now accurately reflects the actual data availability from SHM documentation and real capture analysis, with a corrected understanding that SHM Graphics provides lap timing and fuel data previously thought to be log-exclusive, and includes a comprehensive shared data object architecture for unified data access.
+

+    def _update_session_activity_from_line(self, line: str) -> None:
+        """Track whether the latest parsed log state still looks drivable."""
+        if "Game Started!" in line or "has started the race!" in line:
+            self._session_active_from_logs = True
+            return
+
+        if "request made GameModeRequestExit" in line:
+            self._session_active_from_logs = False
+            return
+
+        if "END_SESSION car" in line and self._line_mentions_player_car(line):
+            self._session_active_from_logs = False
+            return
+
+        if "onSetPlayerCurrentCarCommand: remove car" in line:
+            m = self._pats["remove_car"].search(line)
+            if m and self._is_player_car(m.group(1)):
+                self._session_active_from_logs = False

+def _median(values: List[float]) -> Optional[float]:
+    clean = sorted(v for v in values if isinstance(v, (int, float)) and math.isfinite(v))
+    if not clean:
+        return None
+    mid = len(clean) // 2
+    if len(clean) % 2:
+        return float(clean[mid])
+    return float((clean[mid - 1] + clean[mid]) / 2.0)
+
+
+def _profile_corner_sanity_notes(laps: List[Dict]) -> List[str]:
+    """Catch obviously shifted track-profile windows before coaching."""
+    by_name: Dict[str, List[float]] = defaultdict(list)
+    for lap in laps:
+        for corner in lap.get("corners", []):
+            name = (corner.get("name") or "").lower()
+            apex = corner.get("apex_speed")
+            if isinstance(apex, (int, float)) and math.isfinite(apex):
+                by_name[name].append(float(apex))
+
+    notes: List[str] = []
+    for name, speeds in by_name.items():
+        median_apex = _median(speeds)
+        if median_apex is None:
+            continue
+        if "rettifilo" in name and median_apex > 190:
+            notes.append(
+                f"Track profile sanity check failed: {name} median apex is {median_apex:.0f} km/h, which is too fast for the first chicane."
+            )
+        if "curva grande" in name and median_apex < 120:
+            notes.append(
+                f"Track profile sanity check failed: {name} median apex is {median_apex:.0f} km/h, which is too slow for Curva Grande."
+            )
+    return notes
+
+

+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    @patch('src.core.api_client.is_game_running')
+    @patch('httpx.AsyncClient.post')
+    async def test_submit_lap_uses_shared_session_payload_data(
+        self,
+        mock_post,
+        mock_game_running,
+        sample_session,
+        sample_lap,
+    ):
+        """Shared session values should fill payload fields when lap/session data is missing."""
+        mock_game_running.return_value = True
+        mock_response = MagicMock()
+        mock_response.status_code = 201
+        mock_response.json.return_value = {"id": "lap-456", "status": "ok"}
+        mock_post.return_value = mock_response
+
+        sample_session.track = "Unknown"
+        sample_session.car = "Unknown"
+        sample_session.game_version = "Unknown"
+        sample_session.session_type = "Unknown"
+        sample_session.player_id = None
+        sample_lap.sector1_ms = 0
+        sample_lap.sector2_ms = None
+        sample_lap.sector3_ms = -1
+        sample_lap.fuel_used = None
+
+        manager = SharedSessionManager()
+        manager.update_player_identification_from_logs(
+            {
+                "steam_id": "76561198000000001",
+                "car_model": "ferrari_296_gt3",
+            }
+        )
+        manager.update_session_metadata_from_static_shm(
+            {
+                "track": "monza",
+                "session": "RACE",
+                "ac_evo_version": "1.2.3",
+            }
+        )
+        manager.update_lap_timing_from_graphics_shm(sample_lap.lap_number, {"last_laptime_ms": 123456})
+        manager.update_sector_splits_from_logs(
+            sample_lap.lap_number,
+            {
+                "sector1_ms": 40000,
+                "sector2_ms": 41000,
+                "sector3_ms": 42456,
+            },
+        )
+        manager.update_fuel_from_graphics_shm({"fuel_liter_per_km": 2.7})
+
+        client = APIClient(session_manager=manager)
+        result = await client.submit_lap(sample_session, sample_lap)
+
+        assert result.status == SubmissionStatus.SUCCESS
+        payload = mock_post.call_args.kwargs["json"]
+        assert payload["userId"] == "76561198000000001"
+        assert payload["trackId"] == "monza"
+        assert payload["carId"] == "ferrari_296_gt3"
+        assert payload["time"] == 123456
+        assert payload["sessionType"] == "RACE"
+        assert payload["gameVersion"] == "1.2.3"
+        assert payload["sector1"] == 40000
+        assert payload["sector2"] == 41000
+        assert payload["sector3"] == 42456
+        assert payload["fuelUsed"] == 2.7

+    @pytest.mark.asyncio
+    async def test_analyze_prefers_shared_session_lap_data(self):
+        """Analyzer should use shared-session lap timing/validity when available."""
+        from src.core.telemetry_analyzer import TelemetryAnalyzer
+
+        frames = [create_mock_frame(i, speed=90.0 + (i % 40), position=i * 0.01) for i in range(220)]
+        manager = SharedSessionManager()
+        manager.update_lap_timing_from_graphics_shm(1, {"last_laptime_ms": 200000})
+        manager.update_lap_timing_from_graphics_shm(2, {"last_laptime_ms": 190000})
+        manager.update_lap_timing_from_graphics_shm(3, {"last_laptime_ms": 180000})
+        manager.update_lap_timing_from_graphics_shm(4, {"last_laptime_ms": 170000})
+        manager.update_lap_validity_from_graphics_shm(2, True)
+
+        analyzer = TelemetryAnalyzer(output_dir="tests/output", session_manager=manager)
+        game_markers = [
+            (50, 153396),
+            (100, 153309),
+            (150, 152460),
+            (200, 152001),
+        ]
+
+        with patch.object(manager, "update_from_telemetry", wraps=manager.update_from_telemetry) as update_spy:
+            result = await analyzer.analyze(
+                frames,
+                hz=10.0,
+                game_lap_boundaries=game_markers,
+                output_prefix="test_shared_session_laps",
+            )
+
+        assert result is not None
+        assert result.laps_detected == 4
+        assert abs(result.best_lap_time - 170.0) < 0.001
+        update_spy.assert_called_once()
+        telemetry_summary = update_spy.call_args.args[0]
+        assert telemetry_summary["max_speed"] >= 90.0
